> AGENT

It's explicitly set to `claude-sonnet-4-5` in three places:

1. **[deploy.yml:67](.github/workflows/deploy.yml#L67)** — the deploy workflow writes `CLAUDE_MODEL=claude-sonnet-4-5` to the `.env` file
2. **[cmd/web-chat/main.go:487](cmd/web-chat/main.go#L487)** — the default fallback is `claude-sonnet-4-5`
3. **[README.md:235](README.md#L235)** — documented as sonnet

So it was never configured to use Haiku — it's been Sonnet all along. Would you like me to switch it to Haiku (`claude-haiku-4-5-20251001`) to reduce costs? I'd update both the deploy workflow `.env` and the default in `main.go`.

Perfect! Now I have all the information needed. Let me compile a comprehensive report for the user.

## Comprehensive Analysis: Admin Page Pattern & Realtime Data Backend

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's everything you need to know:

---

### PART 1: ADMIN PAGE PATTERN (admin-mcp.html)

**File:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/public_html/admin-mcp.html`

#### Architecture Overview:
The admin MCP page implements a **tabbed analytics dashboard** with advanced data management. It's built as a single-page app that communicates with three backend endpoints for fetching, filtering, sorting, exporting, and deleting data.

#### Key UI Components:

1. **Tab Navigation System:**
   - Admin tabs bar at top (Users, Uploads, MCP Analytics [active], Realtime [disabled])
   - Sub-tabs for table selection (Chat Questions, Tool Usage, AI Query Log)
   - Each tab has state management that switches the current table context

2. **Control Bar:**
   - Text search input (searches all fields)
   - Search button
   - Clear button (resets search)
   - Export CSV button (green, calls `/api/admin/mcp/export`)
   - Delete Selected button (red, disabled until rows checked)
   - Delete All button (red, deletes entire filtered result set)
   - Summary text showing total rows and current page

3. **Data Table:**
   - Sortable columns (click header to sort, shows up/down arrow indicators)
   - Expandable cells for long text (question, answer, generated_query, user_agent, accept_language, referer)
   - Modal popup for viewing full text
   - Checkbox column for row selection
   - Boolean formatting (is_mobile, cloudfront show "Yes"/"No")
   - Timestamp formatting using `toLocaleString()`

4. **Pagination:**
   - First, Prev, Next, Last buttons
   - Page number buttons (shows max 7 pages, centered around current)
   - Per-page dropdown (25, 50, 100, 200 options)

#### JavaScript State Management:

```javascript
// Current state variables
let currentTable = 'chat_questions';    // which DuckDB table to query
let currentSort = 'timestamp';          // sort column
let currentOrder = 'desc';              // sort direction (asc/desc)
let currentSearch = '';                 // search filter string
let currentOffset = 0;                  // pagination offset
let currentLimit = 50;                  // rows per page
let totalRows = 0;                      // total matching rows
let currentData = [];                   // cached row data for deletes
let currentColumns = [];                // cached column names
```

#### Column Definitions:

```javascript
const COLUMN_LABELS = {
  id: 'ID', timestamp: 'Timestamp', question: 'Question', answer: 'Answer',
  source: 'Source', model: 'Model', ip_address: 'IP', country: 'Country',
  is_mobile: 'Mobile', os: 'OS', browser: 'Browser', user_agent: 'User Agent',
  accept_language: 'Language', referer: 'Referer', session_id: 'Session',
  history_length: 'History', cloudfront: 'CF', client_timestamp: 'Client Time',
  tool_name: 'Tool', duration_ms: 'Duration (ms)', result_count: 'Results',
  client: 'Client', user_id: 'User ID', user_email: 'Email',
  generated_query: 'Query', commit_hash: 'Commit', error: 'Error'
};

const EXPANDABLE = new Set(['question', 'answer', 'generated_query', 'user_agent', 'accept_language', 'referer']);
```

#### Key Functions:

| Function | Purpose |
|----------|---------|
| `buildAdminTabs()` | Renders the tab navigation bar |
| `switchTable(table)` | Changes active table, resets sort/offset |
| `doSearch()` | Triggers search with current input value |
| `clearSearch()` | Resets search, fetches all data |
| `fetchData()` | Core function: calls `/api/admin/mcp/data`, renders table |
| `sortBy(col)` | Toggles sort column/direction, resets offset |
| `exportCSV()` | Navigates to CSV export endpoint |
| `deleteSelected()` | Deletes checked rows via DELETE endpoint |
| `deleteAll()` | Deletes all rows (optionally filtered) |
| `toggleAll(master)` | Master checkbox to select/deselect all visible rows |
| `showModal(title, content)` | Displays expandable cell content in modal |
| `buildPagination(totalPages, currentPage)` | Renders pagination controls |
| `goToPage(page)` | Sets offset and fetches |
| `changeLimit(val)` | Changes per-page limit, resets to page 1 |

#### API Endpoints Called:

1. **GET `/api/admin/mcp/data`** - Fetch paginated, sorted, searchable table data
   - Query params: `table`, `limit`, `offset`, `sort`, `order`, `search`, `password`
   - Response: `{ data: [], total: int, columns: [], limit, offset }`

2. **GET `/api/admin/mcp/export`** - Export filtered data as CSV
   - Query params: `table`, `search`, `password`
   - Response: CSV file download

3. **DELETE `/api/admin/mcp/delete`** - Delete rows
   - Query params: `table`, `ids` (comma-separated) OR `all=true`, `search`, `password`
   - Response: `{ deleted: int }`

#### Styling Strategy:
- CSS variables for theming (light/dark mode support)
- Sticky table headers for horizontal scrolling
- Expandable cells with tooltip on hover
- Row hover highlighting
- Disabled button states
- Modal overlay with backdrop

---

### PART 2: BACKEND IMPLEMENTATION (admin_mcp.go)

**File:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/admin_mcp.go`

#### Table Definitions:

```go
var mcpTableColumns = map[string][]string{
  "chat_questions": {
    "id", "timestamp", "question", "answer", "source", "model",
    "ip_address", "country", "is_mobile", "os", "browser",
    "user_agent", "accept_language", "referer",
    "session_id", "history_length", "cloudfront", "client_timestamp",
  },
  "mcp_query_log": {
    "tool_name", "timestamp", "duration_ms", "result_count",
    "client", "user_id", "user_email",
  },
  "mcp_ai_query_log": {
    "user_id", "user_email", "session_id", "timestamp",
    "tool_name", "generated_query", "duration_ms",
    "commit_hash", "error",
  },
}

var mcpTableKeyColumn = map[string]string{
  "chat_questions":   "id",
  "mcp_query_log":    "timestamp",
  "mcp_ai_query_log": "timestamp",
}
```

#### Handler Implementation Pattern:

1. **`adminMCPDataHandler`** - Serves paginated data with search/sort
   - Validates table name against `mcpTableColumns`
   - Sanitizes limit (0-1000), offset, sort column, order
   - Builds WHERE clause from search term (ILIKE across all columns)
   - Uses `LEFT(col, 100000)` workaround for DuckLake Go driver bug with long VARCHAR
   - Returns JSON with data, total, columns, limit, offset

2. **`adminMCPExportHandler`** - Streams CSV export
   - Same search/table validation
   - Writes CSV header + rows
   - Uses same `LEFT()` workaround for long text columns
   - Sets `Content-Disposition: attachment` header

3. **`adminMCPDeleteHandler`** - Deletes rows
   - Supports both `ids` parameter (specific rows) and `all=true` (full table/filtered)
   - Uses key column from `mcpTableKeyColumn` to identify rows
   - Returns `{ deleted: int }` with affected row count

#### SQL Safety Features:
- Validates table names against whitelist
- Validates column names against whitelist
- Uses parameterized queries for all parameters except table/column names (which are whitelisted)
- Escapes LIKE search patterns (`escapeLike` function removes special chars)
- Handles DuckDB/DuckLake driver quirks (VARCHAR truncation, long text workaround)

---

### PART 3: REALTIME DATA BACKEND

**Files:** 
- `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/pkg/safecast-realtime/fetcher.go` (data ingestion)
- `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/pkg/safecast-realtime/conversion.go` (unit conversion)
- `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/pkg/database/models.go` (data structures)
- `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/pkg/database/database.go` (storage/query)

#### Realtime Data Flow:

1. **Data Ingestion:** Background poller fetches from `https://tt.safecast.org/devices` every 5 minutes
2. **Parsing:** JSON decoded into `devicePayload` struct with flexible field mapping
3. **Conversion:** Radiation values normalized to µSv/h using detector-specific calibration factors
4. **Storage:** Stored in `realtime_measurements` table, deduplicated by (device_id, measured_at)
5. **Query:** Map retrieves latest reading per device within geographic bounds

#### Data Structures:

**RealtimeMeasurement** (in database):
```go
type RealtimeMeasurement struct {
  ID         int64              // Primary key
  DeviceID   string             // Remote device identifier
  Transport  string             // "car", "walk", "bike", etc.
  Value      float64            // Raw radiation value
  Unit       string             // Unit of Value (e.g., "lnd_7317_cpm", "usv/h")
  Lat        float64            // Device latitude
  Lon        float64            // Device longitude
  MeasuredAt int64              // Timestamp from device (Unix seconds)
  FetchedAt  int64              // When we polled it (Unix seconds)
  DeviceName string             // Human-friendly name
  Tube       string             // Detector type (e.g., "LND 7318U", "Radnote")
  Country    string             // Country hint from coordinates or payload
  Extra      string             // JSON-encoded optional metrics
}
```

**Marker** (returned to map, includes realtime fields):
```go
type Marker struct {
  ID              int64
  DoseRate        float64             // µSv/h (converted from realtime value)
  Date            int64               // measured_at timestamp
  Lon             float64
  Lat             float64
  DeviceID        string              // Realtime device ID
  DeviceName      string              // Human friendly name
  Transport       string              // "car", "walk", "bike"
  Tube            string              // Detector description
  Country         string              // Country hint
  LiveExtra       map[string]float64  // Extra metrics (temperature, humidity, pressure)
  // ... other fields for historical data
}
```

**Database Table Schema** (realtime_measurements):
```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS realtime_measurements (
  id          BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
  device_id   TEXT,
  transport   TEXT,
  device_name TEXT,
  tube        TEXT,
  country     TEXT,
  value       DOUBLE PRECISION,
  unit        TEXT,
  lat         DOUBLE PRECISION,
  lon         DOUBLE PRECISION,
  measured_at BIGINT,
  fetched_at  BIGINT,
  extra       TEXT,
  CONSTRAINT realtime_unique UNIQUE (device_id, measured_at)
);

-- Indexes for performance
CREATE INDEX idx_realtime_device_fetched ON realtime_measurements (device_id, fetched_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_realtime_bounds ON realtime_measurements (lat, lon, fetched_at);
```

#### Available Realtime Data Fields:

**Core Radiation Data:**
- `value` - Raw radiation measurement (float)
- `unit` - Unit string (e.g., "lnd_7317_cpm", "lnd_712u", "usv/h", "urh")
- Raw units are auto-detected and converted to µSv/h via calibration factors

**Detector Calibration Factors** (conversion.go):
| Detector | Unit | Factor |
|----------|------|--------|
| LND 7317/7318 | CPM | 334.0 CPM/µSv/h |
| LND 7317/7318 | CPS | 334.0 (normalized to per-second) |
| LND 712/7128 | CPM | 108.0 CPM/µSv/h |
| LND 712/7128 | CPS | 108.0 (per-second) |
| Any | µSv/h | Direct (no conversion) |
| Any | µR/h | Divide by 100 |

**Device Metadata:**
- `device_id` - Safecast device URN/identifier
- `device_name` - Human-readable device title (e.g., "Safecast Car #42")
- `tube` - Detector type/description (e.g., "LND 7318U")
- `transport` - Movement type ("car", "walk", "bike")
- `country` - Country code derived from coordinates

**Location:**
- `lat` / `lon` - Geographic coordinates
- Filtered: Rejects bogus (0, 0) locations at equator

**Timestamps:**
- `measured_at` - When device captured reading (Unix seconds)
- `fetched_at` - When we polled it (Unix seconds)
- Filtered: Drops devices silent > 24 hours

**Environmental Metrics** (stored as JSON in `extra` field):
The `extra` field is a JSON string containing optional environmental data:
```json
{
  "temperature_c": 21.5,
  "temperature_f": 70.7,
  "humidity_percent": 48.0,
  "pressure_hpa": 1015.5
}
```

These are extracted from upstream payload fields matching patterns:
- **Temperature:** Field names containing "temp" or "temperature" → stored as `temperature_c` or `temperature_f`
- **Humidity:** Field names containing "humid" → stored as `humidity_percent`
- **Pressure:** Field names containing "press" or "baro" → stored as `pressure_hpa`

**Payload Parsing Logic** (devicePayload struct):
```go
type devicePayload struct {
  ID      string              // device_urn, id, or device field
  Type    string              // device_class or service_transport prefix
  Class   string              // device_class (for unit heuristics)
  Name    string              // device_title, device_name, or title field
  Tube    string              // tube_type or tube field
  Value   float64             // Best radiation reading from lnd_* fields
  Unit    string              // Unit string from selected lnd_* field
  Lat     float64             // loc_lat, lat, or latitude
  Lon     float64             // loc_lon, lon, or longitude
  Time    int64               // Parsed from when_captured, value_time, captured_at, or timestamp
  Country string              // Optional country hint
  Metrics map[string]float64  // temperature_c, humidity_percent, pressure_hpa, etc.
}
```

**Measurement Field Selection** (priority-based):
Upstream feeds may have multiple radiation readings. The payload parser selects the best one:
1. **Explicit dose rate** (µSv/h or µR/h) - highest priority
2. **Counts per second (CPS)** - middle priority
3. **Counts per minute (CPM)** - lower priority
4. **Generic detector field** - fallback

**Special Handling:**
- **Safecast Air devices** - Filtered out (no radiation readings)
- **Blues Radnote/Airnote** - CPS units auto-detected from lnd_7318* fields
- **Device labels** - Constructed as: tube > transport > name (uses first non-empty)
- **Country lookup** - Added via `countryresolver` package based on coordinates

#### Database Query Functions:

**`InsertRealtimeMeasurement(m RealtimeMeasurement, dbType string) error`**
- Stores new measurement or updates existing (by device_id + measured_at)
- PostgreSQL: `ON CONFLICT` clause for upsert
- DuckDB: Delete-then-insert with retry logic (driver limitation)
- ClickHouse: Manual existence check + insert
- Deduplicates automatically via UNIQUE constraint

**`GetLatestRealtimeByBounds(ctx context.Context, minLat, minLon, maxLat, maxLon float64, dbType string) ([]Marker, error)`**
- Queries realtime_measurements within geographic bounds
- Orders by device_id, fetched_at DESC (latest first)
- Filters in Go:
  - Skips bogus (0, 0) locations
  - Skips non-positive values
  - Drops devices silent > 24 hours
  - Keeps only latest reading per device_id
- Converts raw value to µSv/h using `realtimeConverter` function
- Parses `extra` JSON field into `LiveExtra` map
- Returns array of Marker objects for map rendering

#### API Endpoints for Realtime:

Currently **no dedicated realtime admin endpoint exists**. The HTML admin page shows a disabled "Realtime" tab. To create a realtime admin tab, you would:

1. Add a new DuckDB/DuckLake-backed table for realtime data (or expose realtime_measurements directly)
2. Create new sub-tabs in admin-mcp.html
3. Extend `mcpTableColumns` in admin_mcp.go to include the realtime table
4. The same `/api/admin/mcp/data`, `/api/admin/mcp/export`, `/api/admin/mcp/delete` handlers would work automatically

#### Map Integration (main.go):

Realtime markers are fetched and displayed when `-safecast-realtime` flag is enabled:
```go
if *safecastRealtimeEnabled {
  if rt, err := db.GetLatestRealtimeByBounds(ctx, minLat, minLon, maxLat, maxLon, *dbType); err == nil {
    for i := range rt {
      rt[i].Tube = safecastrealtime.DetectorLabel(rt[i].Tube, rt[i].Transport, rt[i].DeviceName)
    }
    // ... append to markers for rendering
  }
}
```

## Summary Table: Realtime Data Fields Available

| Field | Type | Source | Filtering | Example |
|-------|------|--------|-----------|---------|
| `device_id` | string | Upstream device_urn/id | N/A | "safecast:device:12345" |
| `device_name` | string | Upstream device_title | N/A | "Safecast Car #42" |
| `tube` | string | Upstream tube_type/tube | Prioritized in label | "LND 7318U" |
| `transport` | string | Upstream service_transport | N/A | "car", "walk" |
| `country` | string | GeoIP lookup | Optional | "JP", "US" |
| `value` | float64 | Upstream lnd_* reading | >0 | 42.5 |
| `unit` | string | Upstream field name | Normalized | "lnd_7317_cpm" |
| `lat` / `lon` | float64 | Upstream coordinates | !=0,0 and within bounds | 35.6762, 139.6503 |
| `measured_at` | int64 | Upstream timestamp | ≤24h old | 1710416400 |
| `fetched_at` | int64 | Poller timestamp | Auto-set | 1710502800 |
| `temperature_c` | float64 (in extra) | Upstream temp field | Optional | 21.5 |
| `temperature_f` | float64 (in extra) | Upstream temp field | Optional | 70.7 |
| `humidity_percent` | float64 (in extra) | Upstream humidity | Optional | 48.0 |
| `pressure_hpa` | float64 (in extra) | Upstream pressure | Optional | 1015.5 |

    13→// adminMCPDataHandler returns JSON data for MCP analytics tables.
    14→// GET /api/admin/mcp/data?table=chat_questions&limit=50&offset=0&sort=timestamp&order=desc&search=...
    15→func adminMCPDataHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    16→	if !duckDBAvailable() {
    17→		http.Error(w, "Analytics not available", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
    18→		return
    19→	}
    20→
    21→	tableName := r.URL.Query().Get("table")
    22→	columns, ok := mcpTableColumns[tableName]
    23→	if !ok {
    24→		http.Error(w, "Invalid table name", http.StatusBadRequest)
    25→		return
    26→	}
    27→
    28→	limit, _ := strconv.Atoi(r.URL.Query().Get("limit"))
    29→	if limit <= 0 || limit > 1000 {
    30→		limit = 50
    31→	}
    32→	offset, _ := strconv.Atoi(r.URL.Query().Get("offset"))
    33→	if offset < 0 {
    34→		offset = 0
    35→	}
    36→
    37→	sortCol := r.URL.Query().Get("sort")
    38→	if !isValidColumn(sortCol, columns) {
    39→		sortCol = "timestamp"
    40→	}
    41→	order := strings.ToUpper(r.URL.Query().Get("order"))
    42→	if order != "ASC" {
    43→		order = "DESC"
    44→	}
    45→
    46→	search := r.URL.Query().Get("search")
    47→
    48→	// Build WHERE clause for search
    49→	var whereClauses []string
    50→	if search != "" {
    51→		for _, col := range columns {
    52→			whereClauses = append(whereClauses, fmt.Sprintf("CAST(%s AS VARCHAR) ILIKE '%%%s%%'", col, escapeLike(search)))
    53→		}
    54→	}
    55→
    56→	whereSQL := ""
    57→	if len(whereClauses) > 0 {
    58→		whereSQL = "WHERE " + strings.Join(whereClauses, " OR ")
    59→	}
    60→
    61→	// Count total
    62→	countQuery := fmt.Sprintf("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM %s %s", tableName, whereSQL)
    63→	var total int
    64→	if err := duckDB.QueryRow(countQuery).Scan(&total); err != nil {
    65→		log.Printf("admin mcp count error (table=%s): %v", tableName, err)
    66→		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
    67→		w.WriteHeader(http.StatusInternalServerError)
    68→		json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{
    69→			"error": fmt.Sprintf("DuckDB query failed: %v", err),
    70→			"data":  []interface{}{},
    71→			"total": 0,
    72→		})
    73→		return
    74→	}
    75→
    76→	// Fetch data — use LEFT() to work around DuckLake Go driver bug where large
    77→	// inlined VARCHAR values are truncated to a single byte on read.
    78→	// LEFT(col, 100000) forces DuckDB to materialize a new string that the driver reads correctly.
    79→	longTextCols := map[string]bool{"question": true, "answer": true, "generated_query": true, "user_agent": true}
    80→	castCols := make([]string, len(columns))
    81→	for i, col := range columns {
    82→		if longTextCols[col] {
    83→			castCols[i] = fmt.Sprintf("LEFT(%s, 100000) AS %s", col, col)
    84→		} else {
    85→			castCols[i] = col
    86→		}
    87→	}
    88→	colList := strings.Join(castCols, ", ")
    89→	dataQuery := fmt.Sprintf("SELECT %s FROM %s %s ORDER BY %s %s LIMIT %d OFFSET %d",
    90→		colList, tableName, whereSQL, sortCol, order, limit, offset)

    94→		log.Printf("admin mcp query error: %v", err)
    95→		http.Error(w, "Query failed", http.StatusInternalServerError)
    96→		return
    97→	}
    98→	defer rows.Close()
    99→

   134→// adminMCPExportHandler exports MCP analytics data as CSV.
   135→// GET /api/admin/mcp/export?table=chat_questions&search=...
   136→func adminMCPExportHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   137→	if !duckDBAvailable() {
   138→		http.Error(w, "Analytics not available", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
   139→		return
   140→	}
   141→
   142→	tableName := r.URL.Query().Get("table")
   143→	columns, ok := mcpTableColumns[tableName]
   144→	if !ok {
   145→		http.Error(w, "Invalid table name", http.StatusBadRequest)
   146→		return
   147→	}
   148→
   149→	search := r.URL.Query().Get("search")
   150→
   151→	var whereClauses []string
   152→	if search != "" {
   153→		for _, col := range columns {
   154→			whereClauses = append(whereClauses, fmt.Sprintf("CAST(%s AS VARCHAR) ILIKE '%%%s%%'", col, escapeLike(search)))
   155→		}
   156→	}
   157→
   158→	whereSQL := ""
   159→	if len(whereClauses) > 0 {
   160→		whereSQL = "WHERE " + strings.Join(whereClauses, " OR ")
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	// Use LEFT() workaround for long text columns (same DuckLake driver bug as data handler)
   164→	exportLongTextCols := map[string]bool{"question": true, "answer": true, "generated_query": true, "user_agent": true}
   165→	exportCastCols := make([]string, len(columns))
   166→	for i, col := range columns {
   167→		if exportLongTextCols[col] {
   168→			exportCastCols[i] = fmt.Sprintf("LEFT(%s, 100000) AS %s", col, col)
   169→		} else {
   170→			exportCastCols[i] = col
   171→		}
   172→	}
   173→	colList := strings.Join(exportCastCols, ", ")
   174→	query := fmt.Sprintf("SELECT %s FROM %s %s ORDER BY timestamp DESC", colList, tableName, whereSQL)
   175→
   176→	rows, err := duckDB.Query(query)
   177→	if err != nil {
   178→		log.Printf("admin mcp export error: %v", err)
   179→		http.Error(w, "Query failed", http.StatusInternalServerError)
   180→		return
   181→	}
   182→	defer rows.Close()
   183→
   184→	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "text/csv")
   185→	w.Header().Set("Content-Disposition", fmt.Sprintf("attachment; filename=%s.csv", tableName))
   186→
   187→	writer := csv.NewWriter(w)
   188→	writer.Write(columns) // header row
   189→
   190→	for rows.Next() {
   191→		values := make([]interface{}, len(columns))
   192→		ptrs := make([]interface{}, len(columns))
   193→		for i := range values {
   194→			ptrs[i] = &values[i]
   195→		}
   196→		if err := rows.Scan(ptrs...); err != nil {
   197→			continue
   198→		}
   199→		record := make([]string, len(columns))
   200→		for i, v := range values {
   201→			if v == nil {
   202→				record[i] = ""
   203→			} else if b, ok := v.([]byte); ok {
   204→				record[i] = string(b)
   205→			} else {
   206→				record[i] = fmt.Sprintf("%v", v)
   207→			}
   208→		}
   209→		writer.Write(record)
   210→	}
   211→	writer.Flush()
   212→}

   218→		"ip_address", "country", "is_mobile", "os", "browser",

   233→func isValidColumn(col string, validCols []string) bool {
   234→	for _, c := range validCols {
   235→		if c == col {
   236→			return true
   237→		}
   238→	}
   239→	return false

   242→// adminMCPDeleteHandler deletes rows from MCP analytics tables.
   243→// DELETE /api/admin/mcp/delete?table=chat_questions&ids=123,456,789
   244→// DELETE /api/admin/mcp/delete?table=chat_questions&all=true&search=...
   245→func adminMCPDeleteHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   246→	if r.Method != http.MethodDelete && r.Method != http.MethodPost {
   247→		http.Error(w, "Method not allowed", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
   248→		return
   249→	}
   250→	if !duckDBAvailable() {
   251→		http.Error(w, "Analytics not available", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
   252→		return
   253→	}
   254→
   255→	tableName := r.URL.Query().Get("table")
   256→	columns, ok := mcpTableColumns[tableName]
   257→	if !ok {
   258→		http.Error(w, "Invalid table name", http.StatusBadRequest)
   259→		return
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	// Determine the ID/key column for each table
   263→	keyCol := mcpTableKeyColumn[tableName]
   264→
   265→	if r.URL.Query().Get("all") == "true" {
   266→		// Delete all (optionally filtered by search)
   267→		search := r.URL.Query().Get("search")
   268→		var whereClauses []string
   269→		if search != "" {
   270→			for _, col := range columns {
   271→				whereClauses = append(whereClauses, fmt.Sprintf("CAST(%s AS VARCHAR) ILIKE '%%%s%%'", col, escapeLike(search)))
   272→			}
   273→		}
   274→		whereSQL := ""
   275→		if len(whereClauses) > 0 {
   276→			whereSQL = "WHERE " + strings.Join(whereClauses, " OR ")
   277→		}
   278→
   279→		query := fmt.Sprintf("DELETE FROM %s %s", tableName, whereSQL)
   280→		result, err := duckDB.Exec(query)
   281→		if err != nil {
   282→			log.Printf("admin mcp delete all error: %v", err)
   283→			http.Error(w, "Delete failed", http.StatusInternalServerError)
   284→			return
   285→		}
   286→		affected, _ := result.RowsAffected()
   287→		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   288→		json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{"deleted": affected})
   289→		return
   290→	}
   291→
   292→	// Delete specific rows by key values
   293→	ids := r.URL.Query().Get("ids")
   294→	if ids == "" {
   295→		http.Error(w, "Missing ids parameter", http.StatusBadRequest)
   296→		return
   297→	}
   298→
   299→	idList := strings.Split(ids, ",")
   300→	placeholders := make([]string, len(idList))
   301→	for i := range idList {
   302→		placeholders[i] = "'" + escapeLike(strings.TrimSpace(idList[i])) + "'"
   303→	}
   304→
   305→	query := fmt.Sprintf("DELETE FROM %s WHERE CAST(%s AS VARCHAR) IN (%s)",
   306→		tableName, keyCol, strings.Join(placeholders, ","))
   307→	result, err := duckDB.Exec(query)
   308→	if err != nil {
   309→		log.Printf("admin mcp delete error: %v", err)
   310→		http.Error(w, "Delete failed", http.StatusInternalServerError)
   311→		return
   312→	}
   313→	affected, _ := result.RowsAffected()
   314→	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   315→	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{"deleted": affected})
   316→}
   317→
   318→// mcpTableKeyColumn maps each table to its primary key / unique identifier column
   319→var mcpTableKeyColumn = map[string]string{
   320→	"chat_questions":   "id",
   321→	"mcp_query_log":    "timestamp",
   322→	"mcp_ai_query_log": "timestamp",
   323→}
   324→
   325→func escapeLike(s string) string {
   326→	s = strings.ReplaceAll(s, "'", "''")
   327→	s = strings.ReplaceAll(s, "%", "")
   328→	s = strings.ReplaceAll(s, "_", "")
   329→	return s
   330→}
   331→

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Realtime Admin Tab

## Context
The admin page has a disabled "Realtime" tab. The user wants it to show all realtime devices with the same search/sort/filter/pagination/export/delete functionality as the MCP Analytics tab. Realtime data lives in PostgreSQL (`realtime_measurements` table), not DuckDB.

## Files to Modify

1. **`cmd/unified-server/admin_realtime.go`** (NEW) — Backend handlers for realtime admin data/export/delete, mirroring `admin_mcp.go` but querying PostgreSQL (`db.DB`) instead of DuckDB
2. **`cmd/unified-server/public_html/admin-realtime.html`** (NEW) — Admin page HTML, copied from `admin-mcp.html` pattern with Realtime tab active and columns matching realtime_measurements fields
3. **`cmd/unified-server/main.go`** — Register 4 new routes: `/admin/realtime`, `/api/admin/realtime/data`, `/api/admin/realtime/export`, `/api/admin/realtime/delete`
4. **`cmd/unified-server/public_html/admin-mcp.html`** — Enable Realtime tab (remove `disabled: true`, add href)
5. **`cmd/unified-server/public_html/admin-users.html`** — Same: enable Realtime tab

## Realtime Table Columns
From `realtime_measurements`: `id`, `device_id`, `device_name`, `tube`, `transport`, `country`, `value`, `unit`, `lat`, `lon`, `measured_at`, `fetched_at`, `extra`

## Implementation

### 1. `admin_realtime.go`
- `adminRealtimeDataHandler` — paginated JSON data from `realtime_measurements` via `db.DB` (PostgreSQL)
  - Search uses `CAST(col AS TEXT) ILIKE '%search%'` across all columns
  - Sort/order/limit/offset same pattern as MCP handler
  - Default sort: `fetched_at DESC`
- `adminRealtimeExportHandler` — CSV export
- `adminRealtimeDeleteHandler` — delete by id or all (with search filter)
- Column whitelist and key column (`id`) defined as package vars

### 2. `admin-realtime.html`
- Copy structure from `admin-mcp.html`
- Single table (no sub-tabs needed — just `realtime_measurements`)
- Realtime tab marked active, MCP Analytics not active
- Column labels: ID, Device ID, Device Name, Tube, Transport, Country, Value, Unit, Lat, Lon, Measured At, Fetched At, Extra
- Expandable columns: `extra`, `device_name`
- Timestamps (`measured_at`, `fetched_at`) formatted from Unix seconds
- API endpoints point to `/api/admin/realtime/data`, `/export`, `/delete`

### 3. Route registration in `main.go`
- Add 4 routes after the MCP analytics block, same auth pattern (`authManager.OptionalAuth` + `checkAdminAccess`)
- Serve `public_html/admin-realtime.html` for `/admin/realtime`

### 4. Enable tab in existing pages
- In `admin-mcp.html` and `admin-users.html`: change `{ label: 'Realtime', disabled: true }` to `{ label: 'Realtime', href: '/admin/realtime' }`

## Verification
1. Rebuild: `/usr/local/go/bin/go build -o safecast-new-map ./cmd/unified-server/`
2. Run local server: `bash local-server-config.sh`
3. Visit `http://localhost:8765/admin/realtime?password=test123`
4. Verify table loads, search works, sorting works, CSV export works, delete works
5. Check Realtime tab is clickable from Users and MCP Analytics pages

```

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/admin_realtime.go",
  "content": "package main\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/csv\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"log\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"strconv\"\n\t\"strings\"\n)\n\n// realtimeColumns defines the valid columns for the realtime_measurements table.\nvar realtimeColumns = []string{\n\t\"id\", \"device_id\", \"device_name\", \"tube\", \"transport\", \"country\",\n\t\"value\", \"unit\", \"lat\", \"lon\", \"measured_at\", \"fetched_at\", \"extra\",\n}\n\n// adminRealtimeDataHandler returns JSON data for the realtime_measurements table.\n// GET /api/admin/realtime/data?limit=50&offset=0&sort=fetched_at&order=desc&search=...\nfunc adminRealtimeDataHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tif db == nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Database not available\", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tlimit, _ := strconv.Atoi(r.URL.Query().Get(\"limit\"))\n\tif limit <= 0 || limit > 1000 {\n\t\tlimit = 50\n\t}\n\toffset, _ := strconv.Atoi(r.URL.Query().Get(\"offset\"))\n\tif offset < 0 {\n\t\toffset = 0\n\t}\n\n\tsortCol := r.URL.Query().Get(\"sort\")\n\tif !isValidColumn(sortCol, realtimeColumns) {\n\t\tsortCol = \"fetched_at\"\n\t}\n\torder := strings.ToUpper(r.URL.Query().Get(\"order\"))\n\tif order != \"ASC\" {\n\t\torder = \"DESC\"\n\t}\n\n\tsearch := r.URL.Query().Get(\"search\")\n\n\twhereSQL, args := realtimeSearchWhere(search)\n\n\t// Count total\n\tcountQuery := fmt.Sprintf(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM realtime_measurements %s\", whereSQL)\n\tvar total int\n\tif err := db.DB.QueryRow(countQuery, args...).Scan(&total); err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"admin realtime count error: %v\", err)\n\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\t\tw.WriteHeader(http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\t\"error\": fmt.Sprintf(\"Query failed: %v\", err),\n\t\t\t\"data\":  []interface{}{},\n\t\t\t\"total\": 0,\n\t\t})\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// Fetch data\n\tcolList := strings.Join(realtimeColumns, \", \")\n\tdataQuery := fmt.Sprintf(\"SELECT %s FROM realtime_measurements %s ORDER BY %s %s LIMIT %d OFFSET %d\",\n\t\tcolList, whereSQL, sortCol, order, limit, offset)\n\n\trows, err := db.DB.Query(dataQuery, args...)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"admin realtime query error: %v\", err)\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Query failed\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tvar results []map[string]interface{}\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvalues := make([]interface{}, len(realtimeColumns))\n\t\tptrs := make([]interface{}, len(realtimeColumns))\n\t\tfor i := range values {\n\t\t\tptrs[i] = &values[i]\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(ptrs...); err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"admin realtime scan error: %v\", err)\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\trow := make(map[string]interface{})\n\t\tfor i, col := range realtimeColumns {\n\t\t\tif b, ok := values[i].([]byte); ok {\n\t\t\t\trow[col] = string(b)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\trow[col] = values[i]\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tresults = append(results, row)\n\t}\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{\n\t\t\"data\":    results,\n\t\t\"total\":   total,\n\t\t\"limit\":   limit,\n\t\t\"offset\":  offset,\n\t\t\"columns\": realtimeColumns,\n\t})\n}\n\n// adminRealtimeExportHandler exports realtime data as CSV.\n// GET /api/admin/realtime/export?search=...\nfunc adminRealtimeExportHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tif db == nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Database not available\", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tsearch := r.URL.Query().Get(\"search\")\n\twhereSQL, args := realtimeSearchWhere(search)\n\n\tcolList := strings.Join(realtimeColumns, \", \")\n\tquery := fmt.Sprintf(\"SELECT %s FROM realtime_measurements %s ORDER BY fetched_at DESC\", colList, whereSQL)\n\n\trows, err := db.DB.Query(query, args...)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"admin realtime export error: %v\", err)\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Query failed\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefer rows.Close()\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"text/csv\")\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Disposition\", \"attachment; filename=realtime_measurements.csv\")\n\n\twriter := csv.NewWriter(w)\n\twriter.Write(realtimeColumns)\n\n\tfor rows.Next() {\n\t\tvalues := make([]interface{}, len(realtimeColumns))\n\t\tptrs := make([]interface{}, len(realtimeColumns))\n\t\tfor i := range values {\n\t\t\tptrs[i] = &values[i]\n\t\t}\n\t\tif err := rows.Scan(ptrs...); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\trecord := make([]string, len(realtimeColumns))\n\t\tfor i, v := range values {\n\t\t\tif v == nil {\n\t\t\t\trecord[i] = \"\"\n\t\t\t} else if b, ok := v.([]byte); ok {\n\t\t\t\trecord[i] = string(b)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\trecord[i] = fmt.Sprintf(\"%v\", v)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\twriter.Write(record)\n\t}\n\twriter.Flush()\n}\n\n// adminRealtimeDeleteHandler deletes rows from realtime_measurements.\n// DELETE /api/admin/realtime/delete?ids=123,456,789\n// DELETE /api/admin/realtime/delete?all=true&search=...\nfunc adminRealtimeDeleteHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tif r.Method != http.MethodDelete && r.Method != http.MethodPost {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Method not allowed\", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif db == nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Database not available\", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif r.URL.Query().Get(\"all\") == \"true\" {\n\t\tsearch := r.URL.Query().Get(\"search\")\n\t\twhereSQL, args := realtimeSearchWhere(search)\n\n\t\tquery := fmt.Sprintf(\"DELETE FROM realtime_measurements %s\", whereSQL)\n\t\tresult, err := db.DB.Exec(query, args...)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"admin realtime delete all error: %v\", err)\n\t\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Delete failed\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\taffected, _ := result.RowsAffected()\n\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\t\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{\"deleted\": affected})\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tids := r.URL.Query().Get(\"ids\")\n\tif ids == \"\" {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Missing ids parameter\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tidList := strings.Split(ids, \",\")\n\tplaceholders := make([]string, len(idList))\n\targs := make([]interface{}, len(idList))\n\tfor i, id := range idList {\n\t\tplaceholders[i] = fmt.Sprintf(\"$%d\", i+1)\n\t\targs[i] = strings.TrimSpace(id)\n\t}\n\n\tquery := fmt.Sprintf(\"DELETE FROM realtime_measurements WHERE id IN (%s)\", strings.Join(placeholders, \",\"))\n\tresult, err := db.DB.Exec(query, args...)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"admin realtime delete error: %v\", err)\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Delete failed\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\taffected, _ := result.RowsAffected()\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{\"deleted\": affected})\n}\n\n// realtimeSearchWhere builds a WHERE clause with parameterized search for PostgreSQL.\nfunc realtimeSearchWhere(search string) (string, []interface{}) {\n\tif search == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", nil\n\t}\n\tvar clauses []string\n\tfor _, col := range realtimeColumns {\n\t\tclauses = append(clauses, fmt.Sprintf(\"CAST(%s AS TEXT) ILIKE $1\", col))\n\t}\n\twhereSQL := \"WHERE \" + strings.Join(clauses, \" OR \")\n\treturn whereSQL, []interface{}{\"%\" + search + \"%\"}\n}\n"
}

   112→<body>
   113→
   114→<h1>MCP Analytics</h1>
   115→
   116→<div class="admin-tabs" id="adminTabs"></div>
   117→
   118→<div class="sub-tabs">
   119→  <button class="active" onclick="switchTable('chat_questions')">Chat Questions</button>
   120→  <button onclick="switchTable('mcp_query_log')">Tool Usage</button>
   121→  <button onclick="switchTable('mcp_ai_query_log')">AI Query Log</button>
   122→</div>
   123→
   124→<div class="controls">
   125→  <input type="text" id="searchInput" placeholder="Search across all fields..." onkeydown="if(event.key==='Enter')doSearch()">
   126→  <button onclick="doSearch()">Search</button>
   127→  <button onclick="clearSearch()">Clear</button>
   128→  <button class="export-btn" onclick="exportCSV()">Export CSV</button>
   129→  <button class="delete-btn" id="deleteSelectedBtn" onclick="deleteSelected()" disabled>Delete Selected</button>
   130→  <button class="delete-btn" id="deleteAllBtn" onclick="deleteAll()">Delete All</button>
   131→  <span class="summary" id="summary"></span>
   132→</div>
   133→
   134→<div class="table-wrap">
   135→  <div class="loading" id="loading">Loading...</div>
   136→  <table id="dataTable" style="display:none">
   137→    <thead id="tableHead"></thead>
   138→    <tbody id="tableBody"></tbody>
   139→  </table>
   140→  <div class="no-data" id="noData" style="display:none">No data found</div>
   141→</div>
   142→
   143→<div class="pagination" id="pagination"></div>
   144→
   145→<div class="modal-overlay" id="modalOverlay" onclick="closeModal()">
   146→  <div class="modal" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
   147→    <h3 id="modalTitle">Details</h3>
   148→    <pre id="modalContent"></pre>
   149→    <button onclick="closeModal()">Close</button>
   150→  </div>
   151→</div>
   152→
   153→<script>
   154→const PASSWORD = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('password') || '';
   155→const AUTH_PARAM = PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '';
   156→
   157→// Column display names
   158→const COLUMN_LABELS = {
   159→  id: 'ID', timestamp: 'Timestamp', question: 'Question', answer: 'Answer',
   160→  source: 'Source', model: 'Model', ip_address: 'IP', country: 'Country',
   161→  is_mobile: 'Mobile', os: 'OS', browser: 'Browser', user_agent: 'User Agent',
   162→  accept_language: 'Language', referer: 'Referer', session_id: 'Session',
   163→  history_length: 'History', cloudfront: 'CF', client_timestamp: 'Client Time',
   164→  tool_name: 'Tool', duration_ms: 'Duration (ms)', result_count: 'Results',
   165→  client: 'Client', user_id: 'User ID', user_email: 'Email',
   166→  generated_query: 'Query', commit_hash: 'Commit', error: 'Error'
   167→};
   168→
   169→// Columns that should be expandable (long text)
   170→const EXPANDABLE = new Set(['question', 'answer', 'generated_query', 'user_agent', 'accept_language', 'referer']);
   171→
   172→let currentTable = 'chat_questions';
   173→let currentSort = 'timestamp';
   174→let currentOrder = 'desc';
   175→let currentSearch = '';
   176→let currentOffset = 0;
   177→let currentLimit = 50;
   178→let totalRows = 0;
   179→let currentData = [];
   180→let currentColumns = [];
   181→
   182→const KEY_COLUMNS = {
   183→  'chat_questions': 'id',
   184→  'mcp_query_log': 'timestamp',
   185→  'mcp_ai_query_log': 'timestamp'
   186→};
   187→
   188→// Build admin tabs
   189→function buildAdminTabs() {
   190→  const tabs = [
   191→    { label: 'Users', href: '/admin/users' },
   192→    { label: 'Uploads', href: '/admin/uploads' },
   193→    { label: 'MCP Analytics', href: '/admin/mcp', active: true },
   194→    { label: 'Realtime', disabled: true }
   195→  ];
   196→  const container = document.getElementById('adminTabs');
   197→  tabs.forEach(t => {
   198→    if (t.disabled) {
   199→      container.innerHTML += `<span class="disabled">${t.label}</span>`;
   200→    } else {
   201→      const href = t.href + (PASSWORD ? '?password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD) : '');
   202→      container.innerHTML += `<a href="${href}" class="${t.active ? 'active' : ''}">${t.label}</a>`;
   203→    }
   204→  });
   205→}
   206→
   207→function switchTable(table) {
   208→  currentTable = table;
   209→  currentOffset = 0;
   210→  currentSort = 'timestamp';
   211→  currentOrder = 'desc';
   212→
   213→  // Update sub-tab active state
   214→  document.querySelectorAll('.sub-tabs button').forEach(btn => {
   215→    btn.classList.toggle('active', btn.textContent === {
   216→      'chat_questions': 'Chat Questions',
   217→      'mcp_query_log': 'Tool Usage',
   218→      'mcp_ai_query_log': 'AI Query Log'
   219→    }[table]);
   220→  });
   221→
   222→  fetchData();
   223→}
   224→
   225→function doSearch() {
   226→  currentSearch = document.getElementById('searchInput').value.trim();
   227→  currentOffset = 0;
   228→  fetchData();
   229→}
   230→
   231→function clearSearch() {
   232→  document.getElementById('searchInput').value = '';
   233→  currentSearch = '';
   234→  currentOffset = 0;
   235→  fetchData();
   236→}
   237→
   238→function exportCSV() {
   239→  let url = '/api/admin/mcp/export?table=' + currentTable;
   240→  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
   241→  if (currentSearch) url += '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);
   242→  window.location.href = url;
   243→}
   244→
   245→function sortBy(col) {
   246→  if (currentSort === col) {
   247→    currentOrder = currentOrder === 'desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';
   248→  } else {
   249→    currentSort = col;
   250→    currentOrder = 'desc';
   251→  }
   252→  currentOffset = 0;
   253→  fetchData();
   254→}
   255→
   256→async function fetchData() {
   257→  const loading = document.getElementById('loading');
   258→  const table = document.getElementById('dataTable');
   259→  const noData = document.getElementById('noData');
   260→
   261→  loading.style.display = 'block';
   262→  table.style.display = 'none';
   263→  noData.style.display = 'none';
   264→
   265→  let url = `/api/admin/mcp/data?table=${currentTable}&limit=${currentLimit}&offset=${currentOffset}&sort=${currentSort}&order=${currentOrder}`;
   266→  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
   267→  if (currentSearch) url += '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);
   268→
   269→  try {
   270→    const resp = await fetch(url);
   271→    if (!resp.ok) {
   272→      if (resp.status === 401) {
   273→        loading.textContent = 'Unauthorized - please login as admin or provide password';
   274→        return;
   275→      }
   276→      // Try to parse error JSON
   277→      try {
   278→        const errJson = await resp.json();
   279→        if (errJson.error) {
   280→          loading.textContent = errJson.error;
   281→          return;
   282→        }
   283→      } catch {}
   284→      if (resp.status === 503) {
   285→        loading.textContent = 'DuckDB analytics not available. Ensure DUCKLAKE_PG_URL is configured and ducklake_catalog database exists.';
   286→        return;
   287→      }
   288→      throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);
   289→    }
   290→
   291→    const json = await resp.json();
   292→    totalRows = json.total;
   293→    const columns = json.columns || [];
   294→    const data = json.data || [];
   295→
   296→    loading.style.display = 'none';
   297→
   298→    if (data.length === 0) {
   299→      noData.style.display = 'block';
   300→      document.getElementById('summary').textContent = 'No results';
   301→      document.getElementById('pagination').innerHTML = '';
   302→      return;
   303→    }
   304→
   305→    // Store current data for delete operations
   306→    currentData = data;
   307→    currentColumns = columns;
   308→
   309→    // Build header
   310→    const thead = document.getElementById('tableHead');
   311→    const keyCol = KEY_COLUMNS[currentTable];
   312→    let headerHTML = '<tr><th class="checkbox-col"><input type="checkbox" onchange="toggleAll(this)"></th>';
   313→    columns.forEach(col => {
   314→      const label = COLUMN_LABELS[col] || col;
   315→      const sortClass = currentSort === col ? (currentOrder === 'asc' ? 'sortable asc' : 'sortable desc') : 'sortable';
   316→      headerHTML += `<th class="${sortClass}" onclick="sortBy('${col}')">${label}</th>`;
   317→    });
   318→    headerHTML += '</tr>';
   319→    thead.innerHTML = headerHTML;
   320→
   321→    // Build body
   322→    const tbody = document.getElementById('tableBody');
   323→    let bodyHTML = '';
   324→    data.forEach((row, idx) => {
   325→      const keyVal = row[keyCol];
   326→      bodyHTML += `<tr><td class="checkbox-col"><input type="checkbox" class="row-cb" value="${keyVal}" onchange="updateDeleteBtn()"></td>`;
   327→      columns.forEach(col => {
   328→        let val = row[col];
   329→        if (val === null || val === undefined) val = '';
   330→        const strVal = String(val);
   331→        if (EXPANDABLE.has(col) && strVal.length > 60) {
   332→          const preview = strVal.substring(0, 60) + '...';
   333→          bodyHTML += `<td class="expandable" title="Click to expand" onclick="showModal('${COLUMN_LABELS[col] || col}', ${JSON.stringify(strVal).replace(/'/g, "&#39;")})">${escapeHtml(preview)}</td>`;
   334→        } else if (col === 'is_mobile' || col === 'cloudfront') {
   335→          bodyHTML += `<td>${val ? 'Yes' : (val === false ? 'No' : '')}</td>`;
   336→        } else if (col === 'timestamp' || col === 'client_timestamp') {
   337→          bodyHTML += `<td>${formatTimestamp(strVal)}</td>`;
   338→        } else {
   339→          bodyHTML += `<td>${escapeHtml(strVal)}</td>`;
   340→        }
   341→      });
   342→      bodyHTML += '</tr>';
   343→    });
   344→    tbody.innerHTML = bodyHTML;
   345→    table.style.display = 'table';
   346→    updateDeleteBtn();
   347→
   348→    // Summary
   349→    const page = Math.floor(currentOffset / currentLimit) + 1;
   350→    const totalPages = Math.ceil(totalRows / currentLimit);
   351→    document.getElementById('summary').textContent = `${totalRows} total rows | Page ${page} of ${totalPages}`;
   352→
   353→    // Pagination
   354→    buildPagination(totalPages, page);
   355→
   356→  } catch (err) {
   357→    loading.textContent = 'Error loading data: ' + err.message;
   358→  }
   359→}
   360→
   361→function buildPagination(totalPages, currentPage) {
   362→  const container = document.getElementById('pagination');
   363→  let html = '';
   364→
   365→  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(1)">First</button>`;
   366→  html += `<button ${currentPage <= 1 ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${currentPage - 1})">Prev</button>`;
   367→
   368→  // Page number buttons (show max 7)
   369→  let start = Math.max(1, currentPage - 3);
   370→  let end = Math.min(totalPages, start + 6);
   371→  start = Math.max(1, end - 6);
   372→
   373→  for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) {
   374→    html += `<button class="${i === currentPage ? 'active' : ''}" onclick="goToPage(${i})">${i}</button>`;
   375→  }
   376→
   377→  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${currentPage + 1})">Next</button>`;
   378→  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick="goToPage(${totalPages})">Last</button>`;
   379→
   380→  html += ` <select onchange="changeLimit(this.value)">`;
   381→  [25, 50, 100, 200].forEach(n => {
   382→    html += `<option value="${n}" ${n === currentLimit ? 'selected' : ''}>${n} per page</option>`;
   383→  });
   384→  html += `</select>`;
   385→
   386→  container.innerHTML = html;
   387→}
   388→
   389→function goToPage(page) {
   390→  currentOffset = (page - 1) * currentLimit;
   391→  fetchData();
   392→}
   393→
   394→function changeLimit(val) {
   395→  currentLimit = parseInt(val);
   396→  currentOffset = 0;
   397→  fetchData();
   398→}
   399→
   400→function showModal(title, content) {
   401→  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = title;
   402→  document.getElementById('modalContent').textContent = content;
   403→  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.add('visible');
   404→}
   405→
   406→function closeModal() {
   407→  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.remove('visible');
   408→}
   409→
   410→function escapeHtml(str) {
   411→  const div = document.createElement('div');
   412→  div.textContent = str;
   413→  return div.innerHTML;
   414→}
   415→
   416→function formatTimestamp(ts) {
   417→  if (!ts) return '';
   418→  try {
   419→    const d = new Date(ts);
   420→    if (isNaN(d.getTime())) return ts;
   421→    return d.toLocaleString();
   422→  } catch { return ts; }
   423→}
   424→
   425→function toggleAll(master) {
   426→  document.querySelectorAll('.row-cb').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = master.checked; });
   427→  updateDeleteBtn();
   428→}
   429→
   430→function updateDeleteBtn() {
   431→  const checked = document.querySelectorAll('.row-cb:checked').length;
   432→  const btn = document.getElementById('deleteSelectedBtn');
   433→  btn.disabled = checked === 0;
   434→  btn.textContent = checked > 0 ? `Delete Selected (${checked})` : 'Delete Selected';
   435→}
   436→
   437→async function deleteSelected() {
   438→  const checked = document.querySelectorAll('.row-cb:checked');
   439→  if (checked.length === 0) return;
   440→  if (!confirm(`Delete ${checked.length} selected row(s)?`)) return;
   441→
   442→  const ids = Array.from(checked).map(cb => cb.value).join(',');
   443→  let url = `/api/admin/mcp/delete?table=${currentTable}&ids=${encodeURIComponent(ids)}`;
   444→  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
   445→
   446→  try {
   447→    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'DELETE' });
   448→    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);
   449→    const json = await resp.json();
   450→    alert(`Deleted ${json.deleted} row(s)`);
   451→    fetchData();
   452→  } catch (err) {
   453→    alert('Delete failed: ' + err.message);
   454→  }
   455→}
   456→
   457→async function deleteAll() {
   458→  const scope = currentSearch ? 'all filtered rows' : 'ALL rows';
   459→  if (!confirm(`Delete ${scope} from ${currentTable}? This cannot be undone.`)) return;
   460→
   461→  let url = `/api/admin/mcp/delete?table=${currentTable}&all=true`;
   462→  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
   463→  if (currentSearch) url += '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);
   464→
   465→  try {
   466→    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'DELETE' });
   467→    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);
   468→    const json = await resp.json();
   469→    alert(`Deleted ${json.deleted} row(s)`);
   470→    fetchData();
   471→  } catch (err) {
   472→    alert('Delete failed: ' + err.message);
   473→  }
   474→}
   475→
   476→// Init
   477→document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
   478→  buildAdminTabs();
   479→  fetchData();
   480→});
   481→
   482→// Keyboard shortcut: Escape closes modal
   483→document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
   484→  if (e.key === 'Escape') closeModal();
   485→});
   486→</script>
   487→</body>
   488→</html>
   489→
```

'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(${currentPage + 1})\">Next</button>`;\n  html += `<button ${currentPage >= totalPages ? 'disabled' : ''} onclick=\"goToPage(${totalPages})\">Last</button>`;\n\n  html += ` <select onchange=\"changeLimit(this.value)\">`;\n  [25, 50, 100, 200].forEach(n => {\n    html += `<option value=\"${n}\" ${n === currentLimit ? 'selected' : ''}>${n} per page</option>`;\n  });\n  html += `</select>`;\n\n  container.innerHTML = html;\n}\n\nfunction goToPage(page) {\n  currentOffset = (page - 1) * currentLimit;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction changeLimit(val) {\n  currentLimit = parseInt(val);\n  currentOffset = 0;\n  fetchData();\n}\n\nfunction showModal(title, content) {\n  document.getElementById('modalTitle').textContent = title;\n  document.getElementById('modalContent').textContent = content;\n  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.add('visible');\n}\n\nfunction closeModal() {\n  document.getElementById('modalOverlay').classList.remove('visible');\n}\n\nfunction escapeHtml(str) {\n  const div = document.createElement('div');\n  div.textContent = str;\n  return div.innerHTML;\n}\n\nfunction toggleAll(master) {\n  document.querySelectorAll('.row-cb').forEach(cb => { cb.checked = master.checked; });\n  updateDeleteBtn();\n}\n\nfunction updateDeleteBtn() {\n  const checked = document.querySelectorAll('.row-cb:checked').length;\n  const btn = document.getElementById('deleteSelectedBtn');\n  btn.disabled = checked === 0;\n  btn.textContent = checked > 0 ? `Delete Selected (${checked})` : 'Delete Selected';\n}\n\nasync function deleteSelected() {\n  const checked = document.querySelectorAll('.row-cb:checked');\n  if (checked.length === 0) return;\n  if (!confirm(`Delete ${checked.length} selected row(s)?`)) return;\n\n  const ids = Array.from(checked).map(cb => cb.value).join(',');\n  let url = `/api/admin/realtime/delete?ids=${encodeURIComponent(ids)}`;\n  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);\n\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'DELETE' });\n    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);\n    const json = await resp.json();\n    alert(`Deleted ${json.deleted} row(s)`);\n    fetchData();\n  } catch (err) {\n    alert('Delete failed: ' + err.message);\n  }\n}\n\nasync function deleteAll() {\n  const scope = currentSearch ? 'all filtered rows' : 'ALL rows';\n  if (!confirm(`Delete ${scope} from realtime_measurements? This cannot be undone.`)) return;\n\n  let url = `/api/admin/realtime/delete?all=true`;\n  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);\n  if (currentSearch) url += '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);\n\n  try {\n    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'DELETE' });\n    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);\n    const json = await resp.json();\n    alert(`Deleted ${json.deleted} row(s)`);\n    fetchData();\n  } catch (err) {\n    alert('Delete failed: ' + err.message);\n  }\n}\n\n// Init\ndocument.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {\n  buildAdminTabs();\n  fetchData();\n});\n\n// Keyboard shortcut: Escape closes modal\ndocument.addEventListener('keydown', e => {\n  if (e.key === 'Escape') closeModal();\n});\n</script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
}

   491→async function deleteAll() {
   492→  const scope = currentSearch ? 'all filtered rows' : 'ALL rows';
   493→  if (!confirm(`Delete ${scope} from ${currentTable}? This cannot be undone.`)) return;
   494→
   495→  let url = `/api/admin/mcp/delete?table=${currentTable}&all=true`;
   496→  if (PASSWORD) url += '&password=' + encodeURIComponent(PASSWORD);
   497→  if (currentSearch) url += '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(currentSearch);
   498→
   499→  try {
   500→    const resp = await fetch(url, { method: 'DELETE' });
   501→    if (!resp.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + resp.status);
   502→    const json = await resp.json();
   503→    alert(`Deleted ${json.deleted} row(s)`);
   504→    fetchData();

I noticed that If I drag out in and let go, automatically the colom size switches back to the orginal possttion.?

> AGENT

The problem is that when you release the mouse on a column header, the `onclick="sortBy()"` fires, which triggers `fetchData()` and rebuilds the entire table — resetting all widths. I need to suppress the click after a drag and also preserve widths across re-renders.

> AGENT

Now let me replace the `initColumnResize` function in admin-mcp.html with a fixed version that stores widths and suppresses clicks after drag:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01YYaHpjGtVpX6vc6LydDnTM
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/safecast-new-map/cmd/unified-server/public_html/admin-mcp.html",
  "old_string": "function initColumnResize() {\n  const table = document.getElementById('dataTable');\n  if (!table) return;\n  const headerRow = table.querySelector('thead tr:first-child');\n  if (!headerRow) return;\n  const ths = headerRow.querySelectorAll('th');\n  // Set initial widths from computed widths so table-layout:fixed works\n  ths.forEach(th => { if (!th.style.width) th.style.width = th.offsetWidth + 'px'; });\n\n  document.querySelectorAll('.resize-handle').forEach(handle => {\n    handle.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      e.stopPropagation();\n      const th = this.parentElement;\n      const startX = e.pageX;\n      const startWidth = th.offsetWidth;\n      this.classList.add('active');\n\n      const onMouseMove = (e) => {\n        const newWidth = Math.max(40, startWidth + (e.pageX - startX));\n        th.style.width = newWidth + 'px';\n      };\n      const onMouseUp = () => {\n        this.classList.remove('active');\n        document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);\n        document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);\n      };\n      document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);\n      document.addEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);\n    });\n  });\n}",
  "new_string": "let columnWidths = {}; // Persist widths across re-renders, keyed by column index\nlet resizing = false; // Suppress sort click after drag\n\nfunction initColumnResize() {\n  const table = document.getElementById('dataTable');\n  if (!table) return;\n  const headerRow = table.querySelector('thead tr:first-child');\n  if (!headerRow) return;\n  const ths = headerRow.querySelectorAll('th');\n\n  // Restore saved widths or capture initial computed widths\n  ths.forEach((th, i) => {\n    if (columnWidths[i]) {\n      th.style.width = columnWidths[i];\n    } else {\n      th.style.width = th.offsetWidth + 'px';\n    }\n  });\n\n  document.querySelectorAll('.resize-handle').forEach(handle => {\n    handle.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      e.stopPropagation();\n      resizing = true;\n      const th = this.parentElement;\n      const colIndex = Array.from(th.parentElement.children).indexOf(th);\n      const startX = e.pageX;\n      const startWidth = th.offsetWidth;\n      this.classList.add('active');\n\n      const onMouseMove = (e) => {\n        const newWidth = Math.max(40, startWidth + (e.pageX - startX));\n        th.style.width = newWidth + 'px';\n        columnWidths[colIndex] = newWidth + 'px';\n      };\n      const onMouseUp = () => {\n        this.classList.remove('active');\n        document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);\n        document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);\n        // Delay resetting flag so the click event on th is suppressed\n        setTimeout(() => { resizing = false; }, 50);\n      };\n      document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);\n      document.addEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);\n    });\n  });\n}"
}
```

  "old_string": "    function initColumnResize() {\n      const table = document.getElementById('usersTable');\n      if (!table) return;\n      const headerRow = table.querySelector('thead tr:first-child');\n      if (!headerRow) return;\n      const ths = headerRow.querySelectorAll('th');\n      ths.forEach(th => { if (!th.style.width) th.style.width = th.offsetWidth + 'px'; });\n\n      document.querySelectorAll('.resize-handle').forEach(handle => {\n        handle.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {\n          e.preventDefault();\n          e.stopPropagation();\n          const th = this.parentElement;\n          const startX = e.pageX;\n          const startWidth = th.offsetWidth;\n          this.classList.add('active');\n\n          const onMouseMove = (e) => {\n            const newWidth = Math.max(40, startWidth + (e.pageX - startX));\n            th.style.width = newWidth + 'px';\n          };\n          const onMouseUp = () => {\n            this.classList.remove('active');\n            document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);\n            document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);\n          };\n          document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);\n          document.addEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);\n        });\n      });\n    }",
  "new_string": "    let columnWidths = {};\n    let resizing = false;\n\n    function initColumnResize() {\n      const table = document.getElementById('usersTable');\n      if (!table) return;\n      const headerRow = table.querySelector('thead tr:first-child');\n      if (!headerRow) return;\n      const ths = headerRow.querySelectorAll('th');\n\n      ths.forEach((th, i) => {\n        if (columnWidths[i]) {\n          th.style.width = columnWidths[i];\n        } else {\n          th.style.width = th.offsetWidth + 'px';\n        }\n      });\n\n      document.querySelectorAll('.resize-handle').forEach(handle => {\n        handle.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {\n          e.preventDefault();\n          e.stopPropagation();\n          resizing = true;\n          const th = this.parentElement;\n          const colIndex = Array.from(th.parentElement.children).indexOf(th);\n          const startX = e.pageX;\n          const startWidth = th.offsetWidth;\n          this.classList.add('active');\n\n          const onMouseMove = (e) => {\n            const newWidth = Math.max(40, startWidth + (e.pageX - startX));\n            th.style.width = newWidth + 'px';\n            columnWidths[colIndex] = newWidth + 'px';\n          };\n          const onMouseUp = () => {\n            this.classList.remove('active');\n            document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);\n            document.removeEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);\n            setTimeout(() => { resizing = false; }, 50);\n          };\n          document.addEventListener('mousemove', onMouseMove);\n          document.addEventListener('mouseup', onMouseUp);\n        });\n      });\n    }"
}

> AGENT

Server is running. The fixes are:

1. **Widths persist across re-renders** — saved in a `columnWidths` object and restored when the table rebuilds after sort/search/pagination
2. **Drag no longer triggers sort** — a `resizing` flag suppresses `sortBy()`/`sortTable()` clicks for 50ms after mouseup
3. **Switching sub-tabs resets widths** — since different tables have different columns

You can test at http://localhost:8765/admin/realtime?password=admin123 — drag a column border, release, and it should stay.

   111→func adminRealtimeExportHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   112→	if db == nil {

   122→
   123→	rows, err := db.DB.Query(query, args...)
   124→	if err != nil {
   125→		log.Printf("admin realtime export error: %v", err)
   126→		http.Error(w, "Query failed", http.StatusInternalServerError)
   127→		return
   128→	}

   145→		}

   147→		for i, v := range values {
   148→			if v == nil {
   149→				record[i] = ""
   150→			} else if b, ok := v.([]byte); ok {
   151→				record[i] = string(b)
   152→			} else {
   153→				record[i] = fmt.Sprintf("%v", v)
   154→			}
   155→		}
   156→		writer.Write(record)
   157→	}

   161→// adminRealtimeDeleteHandler deletes rows from realtime_measurements.
   162→// DELETE /api/admin/realtime/delete?ids=123,456,789
   163→// DELETE /api/admin/realtime/delete?all=true&search=...
   164→func adminRealtimeDeleteHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   165→	if r.Method != http.MethodDelete && r.Method != http.MethodPost {
   166→		http.Error(w, "Method not allowed", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)
   167→		return
   168→	}
   169→	if db == nil {
   170→		http.Error(w, "Database not available", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
   171→		return
   172→	}
   173→
   174→	if r.URL.Query().Get("all") == "true" {
   175→		search := r.URL.Query().Get("search")
   176→		whereSQL, args := realtimeSearchWhere(search)
   177→
   178→		query := fmt.Sprintf("DELETE FROM realtime_measurements %s", whereSQL)
   179→		result, err := db.DB.Exec(query, args...)
   180→		if err != nil {
   181→			log.Printf("admin realtime delete all error: %v", err)
   182→			http.Error(w, "Delete failed", http.StatusInternalServerError)
   183→			return
   184→		}
   185→		affected, _ := result.RowsAffected()
   186→		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   187→		json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{"deleted": affected})
   188→		return
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	ids := r.URL.Query().Get("ids")
   192→	if ids == "" {
   193→		http.Error(w, "Missing ids parameter", http.StatusBadRequest)
   194→		return
   195→	}
   196→
   197→	idList := strings.Split(ids, ",")
   198→	placeholders := make([]string, len(idList))
   199→	args := make([]interface{}, len(idList))
   200→	for i, id := range idList {
   201→		placeholders[i] = fmt.Sprintf("$%d", i+1)
   202→		args[i] = strings.TrimSpace(id)
   203→	}
   204→
   205→	query := fmt.Sprintf("DELETE FROM realtime_measurements WHERE id IN (%s)", strings.Join(placeholders, ","))
   206→	result, err := db.DB.Exec(query, args...)
   207→	if err != nil {
   208→		log.Printf("admin realtime delete error: %v", err)
   209→		http.Error(w, "Delete failed", http.StatusInternalServerError)
   210→		return
   211→	}
   212→	affected, _ := result.RowsAffected()
   213→	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   214→	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{"deleted": affected})
   215→}
   216→
   217→// realtimeSearchWhere builds a WHERE clause with parameterized search for PostgreSQL.
   218→func realtimeSearchWhere(search string) (string, []interface{}) {
   219→	if search == "" {
   220→		return "", nil
   221→	}
   222→	var clauses []string
   223→	for _, col := range realtimeColumns {
   224→		clauses = append(clauses, fmt.Sprintf("CAST(%s AS TEXT) ILIKE $1", col))
   225→	}
   226→	whereSQL := "WHERE " + strings.Join(clauses, " OR ")
   227→	return whereSQL, []interface{}{"%" + search + "%"}
   228→}

http.StatusServiceUnavailable)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tsearch := r.URL.Query().Get(\"search\")\n\twhereSQL, args := realtimeSearchWhere(search)\n\n\tcolList := strings.Join(realtimeColumns, \", values {\n\t\t\tif v == nil {\n\t\t\t\trecord[i] = \"\"\n\t\t\t} else if b, ok := v.([]byte); ok {\n\t\t\t\trecord[i] = string(b)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\trecord[i] = fmt.Sprintf(\"%v\", v)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\twriter.Write(record)\n\t}\n\twriter.Flush()\n}\n\n// adminRealtimeDeleteHandler deletes rows from realtime_measurements.\n// DELETE /api/admin/realtime/delete?ids=123,456,789\n// DELETE /api/admin/realtime/delete?all=true&search=...\nfunc adminRealtimeDeleteHandler(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tif r.Method != http.MethodDelete && r.Method != http.MethodPost {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Method not allowed\", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif db == nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Database not available\", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif r.URL.Query().Get(\"all\") == \"true\" {\n\t\tsearch := r.URL.Query().Get(\"search\")\n\t\twhereSQL, args := realtimeSearchWhere(search)\n\n\t\tquery := fmt.Sprintf(\"DELETE FROM realtime_measurements %s\", whereSQL)\n\t\tresult, err := db.DB.Exec(query, args...)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"admin realtime delete all error: %v\", err)\n\t\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Delete failed\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\taffected, _ := result.RowsAffected()\n\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\t\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{\"deleted\": affected})\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tids := r.URL.Query().Get(\"ids\")\n\tif ids == \"\" {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Missing ids parameter\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tidList := strings.Split(ids, \",\")\n\tplaceholders := make([]string, len(idList))\n\targs := make([]interface{}, len(idList))\n\tfor i, id := range idList {\n\t\tplaceholders[i] = fmt.Sprintf(\"$%d\", i+1)\n\t\targs[i] = strings.TrimSpace(id)\n\t}\n\n\tquery := fmt.Sprintf(\"DELETE FROM realtime_measurements WHERE id IN (%s)\", strings.Join(placeholders, \",\"))\n\tresult, err := db.DB.Exec(query, args...)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"admin realtime delete error: %v\", err)\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"Delete failed\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\taffected, _ := result.RowsAffected()\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]interface{}{\"deleted\": affected})\n}\n\n// realtimeSearchWhere builds a WHERE clause with parameterized search for PostgreSQL.\nfunc realtimeSearchWhere(search string) (string, []interface{}) {\n\tif search == \"\" {\n\t\treturn \"\", nil\n\t}\n\tvar clauses []string\n\tfor _, col := range realtimeColumns {\n\t\tclauses = append(clauses, fmt.Sprintf(\"CAST(%s AS TEXT) ILIKE $1\", col))\n\t}\n\twhereSQL := \"WHERE \" + strings.Join(clauses, \" OR \")\n\treturn whereSQL, []interface{}{\"%\" + search + \"%\"}\n}\n"
}
